### [117] [AC Fingerprints of 2D Electron Hydrodynamics: Superdiffusion and Drude Weight Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15737)
*Davis Thuillier,Thomas Scaffidi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 二维清洁费米液体在低频非局域电导中存在超扩散输运，其特征由两个独立指数z=4/3和α=1/3描述，而非单一指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维清洁费米液体中由费米面奇宇称多极形变缓慢弛豫引起的中间层析输运区间的动力学特性，特别是其在有限频率下的反常行为。

Method: 从微观数值计算线性化电子-电子碰撞算符出发，分析低频非局域电导的极点结构，使用Krylov链描述电流弛豫，并探讨窄通道交流输运的测量方法。

Result: 发现低频非局域电导由单一流体动力学极点控制：σ(q,ω)=𝒟(q)/(iω+η_⋆q^z)，其中z=4/3，η_⋆为超扩散粘度。极点残差本身具有尺度依赖性：𝒟(q)∼q^{-α}，α=1/3。这表明动力学特性由两个独立指数而非单一指数描述。

Conclusion: 二维清洁费米液体的动力学反常行为表现为超扩散输运，其特征由两个独立指数z和α共同描述。窄通道交流输运测量为分别测量这两个指数提供了直接途径，揭示了费米面奇宇称多极形变弛豫在动力学中的重要作用。

Abstract: Clean two-dimensional Fermi liquids are now known to exhibit an intermediate tomographic regime, between ballistic and Navier--Stokes transport, caused by the anomalously slow relaxation of parity-odd multipolar deformations of the Fermi surface. Here we show that this anomaly extends to the dynamical realm. Starting from a microscopic numerical evaluation of the linearized electron--electron collision operator, we find that the finite-frequency nonlocal conductivity is controlled at low frequency by a single hydrodynamic pole, $σ(q,ω)=\mathcal{D}(q)/(iω+η_\star q^z)$, with dynamical exponent $z=4/3$ and superdiffusive viscosity $η_\star$. Remarkably, the pole residue itself is scale dependent and obeys $\mathcal{D}(q)\sim q^{-α}$ with $α=1/3$, so the dynamical properties are described by two separate exponents rather than one. We interpret the residue suppression using a Krylov-chain description of current relaxation: as $q$ increases, the longest-lived quasinormal mode ceases to be a nearly pure current excitation and spreads over higher odd angular harmonics. Finally, we show that AC transport in narrow channels provides a direct route to measuring the exponents $z$ and $α$ separately.

</details>


### [118] [Unified gauge-theory description of quantum spin liquids on square-based frustrated lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15745)
*Atanu Maity,Andreas Feuerpfeil,Ronny Thomale,Subir Sachdev,Yasir Iqbal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个统一框架，将不同晶格上的量子自旋液体视为同一量子相，挑战了传统上认为晶格几何和微观对称性决定自旋液体相的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为量子自旋液体对晶格几何、对称性和微观交换模式高度敏感，导致在受挫磁体中出现了大量看似不同的相。作者旨在提供一个统一框架，将这些看似不同的相联系起来。

Method: 采用投影对称群分析，识别出棋盘晶格自旋液体态，该态与方晶格和Shastry-Sutherland晶格自旋液体形成绝热连接的闭合集合。在连续描述中，所有三种情况都源自共同的SU(2) π-通量母态，并由相同的规范理论QED₃描述。

Result: 证明了方晶格、Shastry-Sutherland晶格和棋盘晶格上的自旋-1/2海森堡反铁磁体可以实现单一统一的量子相：无能隙ℤ₂狄拉克量子自旋液体。这些相在晶格层面和连续描述层面都实现了统一。

Conclusion: 量子自旋液体最根本的分类标准不是晶格几何或微观耦合，而是涌现的规范理论及其希格斯结构。不同的受挫晶格可以承载相同的量子相，并表现出相同的禁闭机制，尽管它们的微观对称性存在显著差异。

Abstract: Quantum spin liquids are commonly thought to be highly sensitive to lattice geometry, symmetry, and microscopic exchange patterns, leading to a proliferation of seemingly distinct phases across frustrated magnets. Here, we provide a framework that unifies phases that appear distinct from the viewpoint of this intuition. We postulate that the spin-$\tfrac{1}{2}$ Heisenberg antiferromagnets on the square, Shastry-Sutherland, and checkerboard lattices can realize a single unified quantum phase: a gapless $\mathbb{Z}_2$ Dirac quantum spin liquid, despite their markedly different lattice symmetries. Using a systematic projective symmetry group analysis, we identify a checkerboard spin-liquid state that completes a closed set of adiabatically connected phases linking the well-established square-lattice and Shastry-Sutherland spin liquids. Crucially, we show that this lattice-level unification is mirrored exactly in the continuum description. In all three cases, the spin liquids descend from a common SU(2) $π$-flux parent state and are governed by the same gauge theory, QED$_3$ with two Dirac fermion flavors coupled to two adjoint Higgs fields. As a result, we postulate that the surrounding Néel and valence-bond-solid phases and their confinement transitions admit a unified interpretation within the framework of deconfined quantum criticality. More broadly, our results suggest that quantum spin liquids are most fundamentally classified not by lattice geometry or microscopic couplings, but by the emergent gauge theory and its Higgs structure. Distinct frustrated lattices can thus host the same quantum phase and exhibit the same confinement mechanisms, despite substantial differences in their microscopic symmetries.

</details>


### [119] [Hubbard model at U=$\infty$: Role of single and two-boson fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15790)
*Debanand Sa,Anirban Dutta*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 开发了一个基于正则费米子表示的半解析框架，用于研究强关联电子系统，重点关注U=∞ Hubbard模型，通过运动方程方法计算费米子自能，揭示了扩散阻尼玻色子模式和线性温度依赖的电阻率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究强关联电子系统的物理特性，特别是U=∞ Hubbard模型中的电子行为，理解极端关联条件下电子自能、玻色子模式和输运性质的变化规律。

Method: 采用基于正则费米子表示的半解析框架，使用运动方程方法计算费米子自能，自能包含单玻色子和双玻色子交换过程，玻色子为自生的局域电荷和自旋密度涨落，在d=∞维度下自洽计算电子自能。

Result: 计算的玻色子谱密度显示为扩散阻尼模式并具有长尾；费米子谱密度在ω=0处显示明显的相干峰，其频率导数在有限ω处呈现双峰结构；电阻率在宽温度范围内呈现线性温度依赖，在极低温下过渡到相干费米液体行为。

Conclusion: 该半解析框架成功描述了强关联电子系统的关键特征，包括扩散阻尼玻色子模式、费米子谱密度的相干峰结构以及电阻率的线性温度依赖，为理解极端关联条件下的电子行为提供了理论洞见。

Abstract: We have developed a semi-analytical framework formulated in the canonical fermion representation to investigate strongly correlated electron systems. We consider the U=$\infty$ Hubbard model and used the equation of motion method to calculate the fermion self-energy which has two parts: single and two-boson exchange processes. The emergent bosons here are self-generated local charge and spin-density fluctuations which become strongly time-dependent due to extreme correlations. The computed boson spectral density is a diffusive damped mode with a long tail. The electron self-energy at $d=\infty$ is computed self-consistently. The corresponding fermionic spectral density displays a pronounced coherence peak at $ω=0$, while its frequency derivative develops a two-peak structure at finite $ω$. The resistivity shows a linear temperature dependence over a broad range, crossing over to coherent Fermi-liquid behavior at extremely low temperatures.

</details>


### [120] [Magnetic Imaging of Macroscopic Spin Chirality Flipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15793)
*H. Miao,G. Fabbris,J. Bouaziz,W. R. Meier,P. Mercado Lozano,Y. Choi,J. Strempfer,D. Haskel,S. Blügel,M. Cook,M. Brahlek,H. N. Lee,A. D. Christianson,A. F. May,S. Okamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用共振磁X射线散射成像技术，在EuAl4中发现宏观手性翻转相变和手性记忆效应，揭示了电荷密度波与手性磁畴的耦合机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性是关联和拓扑态的基本组织原理，但在量子磁体中，宏观尺度上自发手性翻转的机制仍不清楚。研究旨在理解手性磁畴如何在宏观尺度上形成和翻转。

Method: 使用空间分辨率为2.5微米的共振磁X射线散射技术，对关联拓扑磁体EuAl4的纠缠自旋、电荷和晶格序进行成像。

Result: 发现了宏观手性翻转相变和显著的手性记忆效应。手性磁畴的分布与底层电荷密度波的形貌相关，表明存在由竞争的手性和向列晶格场产生的涌现手性磁相互作用。

Conclusion: 磁弹性耦合在稳定同手性和拓扑磁态中具有根本重要性。研究揭示了电荷密度波景观如何调控手性磁畴的宏观行为。

Abstract: Chirality is a fundamental organizing principle of correlated and topological states. In quantum magnets, chirality arises from the geometric twisting of spins and serves as an emergent source of Berry curvature and quantum metrics. Although external fields can reversibly tune the spin chirality, understanding how spontaneous reversal occurs on macroscopic length scale remains an unresolved challenge. In this letter, we use resonant magnetic x-ray scattering with 2.5-micron spatial resolution to image intertwined spin, charge, and lattice orders of the correlated topological magnet EuAl4. We uncover a macroscopic chirality flipping transition and a remarkable chiral memory effect. The chiral magnetic domain tracks the landscape of the underlying charge density wave, implicating emergent chiral magnetic interactions arising from competing chiral and nematic lattice fields. Our results reveal the fundamental significance of magnetoelastic coupling in stabilizing homochiral and topological magnetic states.

</details>


### [121] [Anomalous Thermal Transport Reveals Weak First-Order Melting of Charge Density Waves in 2H-TaSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15915)
*Han Huang,Jinghang Dai,Joyce Christiansen-Salameh,Jiyoung Kim,Samual Kielar,Desheng Ma,Noah Schinitzer,Danrui Ni,Gustavo Alvarez,Chen Li,Carla Slebodnick,Mario Medina,Bilal Azhar,Ahmet Alatas,Robert Cava,David Muller,Zhiting Tian*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过热输运研究2H-TaSe2中电荷密度波（CDW）的熔化过程，发现热导率的V形温度依赖关系揭示了传统声子-声子散射无法解释的隐藏涨落


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维量子材料中有序相的熔化过程难以解析，因为相关涨落是动态且电中性的。需要寻找能够探测这些隐藏涨落的敏感探针

Method: 采用热输运测量作为探针，结合电子衍射和X射线衍射技术，并建立将热输运与空间CDW涨落联系起来的唯象模型

Result: 观察到热导率的V形温度依赖关系，电子衍射显示短程周期性晶格畸变持续到至少300K，X射线衍射显示CDW波矢的热滞后现象

Conclusion: 这些结果表明CDW态通过位错和涨落驱动的弱一级相变熔化，揭示了热输运作为探测隐藏CDW涨落的有效工具

Abstract: How ordered phases melt in low-dimensional quantum materials remain difficult to resolve because the relevant fluctuations are dynamic and charge neutral. In this work, we show that thermal transport provides a sensitive probe of these hidden fluctuations in the layered transition metal dichalcogenide 2H-TaSe2. We observe a striking V-shaped temperature dependence of the thermal conductivity that cannot be explained by conventional phonon-phonon scattering. Instead, it originates from scattering by persistent local charge-density-wave (CDW) correlations, consistent with our phenomenological model linking thermal transport to spatial CDW fluctuation. Electron diffraction reveals short-range periodic lattice distortions persisting to at least 300 K, while X-ray diffraction shows thermal hysteresis of the CDW wavevector. Together, these results reveal a dislocation- and fluctuation-driven weak first-order melting of the CDW state.

</details>


### [122] [Theory of Magnetoacoustic Resonance to Probe Multipole Effects Due to a Crystal Field Quartet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16087)
*Mikito Koga,Masashige Matsumoto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种声学驱动共振新方法，用于探测晶体场四重态中的八极矩和通常被磁性隐藏的四极矩自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法难以探测晶体场四重态中的四极矩自由度，因为它们通常被磁性性质所掩盖。需要开发新方法来直接探测这些隐藏的多极矩自由度及其耦合效应。

Method: 利用表面声波在平行于典型晶轴的外加磁场下传播，通过各向异性共振跃迁速率来探测四极矩特征。八极矩效应通过各向异性塞曼分裂调制跃迁速率。还提出了基于Floquet理论的光子辅助磁声共振方法，用于强磁场下的大激发能隙情况。

Result: 该方法能够定量评估四重态四极矩-应变耦合和各向异性八极矩效应。通过多极矩-多极矩相互作用模型，该方法可用于识别四极矩序参量。

Conclusion: 声学驱动共振方法为探测晶体场四重态中的隐藏多极矩自由度提供了有效途径，特别是在强磁场条件下，光子辅助磁声共振方法扩展了该技术的应用范围。

Abstract: We present a new method of acoustically driven resonance that probes octupole degrees of freedom as well as a quadrupole usually hidden by the magnetic properties of a crystal field quartet. A characteristic of the quadrupole is reflected in the anisotropic resonance transition rate, which depends on the propagation direction of a surface acoustic wave under an external magnetic field parallel to a typical crystallographic axis. The transition rate is modulated by the anisotropic Zeeman splitting associated with octupoles. We demonstrate how to obtain information about the quartet quadrupole-strain coupling and evaluate the anisotropic octupole effect quantitatively. We also discuss the applicability of our method to identifying a quadrupole order parameter using a multipole-multipole interaction model. For large excitation energy gaps under strong magnetic fields, we propose a photon-assisted magnetoacoustic resonance formulated on the basis of the Floquet theory.

</details>


### [123] [SU($N$) Quantum Spin Model with Weak and Strong First-Order Néel to Valence-Bond Solid Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16106)
*Ryan Flynn,Anders W. Sandvik*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个SU(N)对称的二维量子自旋模型（X-Q模型），研究了Néel反铁磁态与自发二聚化态之间的基态相变，发现N=2时接近去禁闭量子临界点，但N>2时相变变为强一级相变，这与传统预期相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究SU(N)对称量子自旋模型中基态相变的普适性行为，特别是探索去禁闭量子临界点的存在条件，以及理解微观相互作用如何支持或破坏这种临界现象。

Method: 引入X-Q模型：X项是两个次近邻置换算符的乘积，Q项是两个最近邻单重态投影算符的乘积（类似J-Q模型中的Q项）。使用量子蒙特卡洛模拟研究该模型在不同N值下的相变行为。

Result: 1. N=2时，相变接近去禁闭量子临界点，与J-Q模型类似；2. N>2时，相变变为强一级相变，这与传统预期（增加N通常会减弱不连续性）相反；3. 对于大N，主导相变的X项无法诱导二聚化模式的U(1)涨落，这解释了强一级相变的出现。

Conclusion: X-Q模型的研究揭示了微观相互作用对去禁闭临界性的重要性。X项在相变中占主导地位时，由于无法诱导二聚化模式的U(1)涨落，导致N>2时出现强一级相变，这为理解支持去禁闭临界性的微观机制提供了新见解。

Abstract: We introduce an SU($N$) symmetric two-dimensional quantum spin model, the $X$-$Q$ model, which hosts a ground state transition between Néel antiferromagnetic and spontaneously dimerized states. The $Q$ terms are products of two adjacent singlet projectors on nearest-neighbor sites, as in the often studied $J$-$Q$ model (where $J$ is the Heisenberg exchange), while the $X$ terms are products of two permutation operators on second-neighbor sites. Quantum Monte Carlo simulations reveal close proximity to a deconfined quantum critical point for $N=2$, as in the $J$-$Q$ model. However, for $N>2$ the transition becomes strongly first-order, contrary to conventional expectations that increasing $N$ should weaken discontinuities. We attribute this behavior to the inability of the $X$ term, which dominates at the transition for large $N$, to induce U(1) fluctuations of the dimer pattern. These results provide insights into the microscopic interactions that support deconfined criticality.

</details>


### [124] [Fractionalized anyons in counterflowing Quantum Hall Liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16222)
*Jun-Xiao Hui,T. H. Hansson,Egor Babaev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多组分逆流态理论，其中Laughlin准粒子可以分裂为携带分数电荷和统计角的分数涡旋，存在两个由量子相变分隔的相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拓扑有序系统中准粒子的分数化现象，特别是Laughlin准粒子如何分裂为携带分数电荷和统计角的分数涡旋，探索超越传统Laughlin态的新拓扑序。

Method: 发展了多组分逆流态的理论框架，分析准粒子分数化过程，识别两个不同的量子相及其相变条件。

Result: 发现存在两个相：第一个相中分数化电荷虽然可观测但渐近受限；第二个相中分数化电荷是自由的任意子，其拓扑序与Laughlin态不同。

Conclusion: 多组分逆流态中Laughlin准粒子可以分数化为分数涡旋，存在受限和自由两个相，后者具有不同于Laughlin态的新拓扑序，由量子相变分隔。

Abstract: A key property of topologically ordered systems, such as Quantum Hall states, is the existence of excitations obeying fractional quantum statistics - anyons. We develop a theory for multicomponent counterflow states where an ordinary Laughlin quasiparticle can split into fractional vortices carrying fractions of its charge and statistical angle. There are two phases, separated by a quantum phase transition, where in the first, although observable, the fractionalized charges are asymptotically confined. In the second phase, they are unconfined anyons and the topological order is different from that of the Laughlin state.

</details>


### [125] [Altermagnetic pseudogap from $\frac{t}{U}$ expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16311)
*Rohit Hegde*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过序参量分析发现，掺杂莫特绝缘体中存在由动力学相互作用驱动的均匀交变磁体，位于反铁磁体和空穴掺杂d波超导体之间，通常被认为是赝隙区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究掺杂莫特绝缘体中的量子相，特别是探索反铁磁体和d波超导体之间的中间区域，理解赝隙现象的微观起源，以及寻找可能的新型量子有序态。

Method: 采用序参量分析t/U级数展开，研究掺杂莫特绝缘体的相图，分析交变磁体的稳定性及其向非均匀自旋和电荷序的失稳过程。

Result: 发现均匀交变磁体占据赝隙区域，其亚稳态边界将超导体分为欠掺杂和过掺杂区域，类似于铜氧化物中的T*交叉或转变。交变磁体对位点、键和电流的非均匀自旋和电荷序不稳定，主要失稳模式为π-通量态。

Conclusion: 交变磁体作为掺杂莫特绝缘体中的新量子相，可能为理解赝隙现象提供新视角，其向π-通量态的失稳暗示着从物理现实的微观模型中可能涌现出自旋-电荷液体和量子有序态。

Abstract: Order parameter analysis of the t/U series reveals a uniform altermagnet endemic to the doped Mott insulator, driven by kinetic interactions, occupying a position between the antiferromagnet and hole-doped d-wave superconductor that is normally reserved for the pseudogap. The metastable boundary of the altermagnet punctures and divides the superconductor into underdoped and overdoped regions, reminiscent of the $T^\ast$ crossover or transition in the cuprates. Similarly, the $T_{pair}$ boundary of the superconductor divides the altermagnet, leading to a low temperature phase susceptible to Cooper fluctuations. The altermagnet is unstable to inhomegeneous spin and charge order of sites, bonds, and currents. Its leading instability is to the $π$-flux state, suggesting the possible emergence of spin-charge liquids and quantum ordered states from a physically realistic microscopic model.

</details>


### [126] [A Correlated Route to Antiferromagnetic Spintronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16552)
*Joel Bobadilla,Alberto Camjayi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在共线反铁磁体中实现自旋极化电荷输运的最小化相关驱动机制，通过掺杂打破粒子-空穴对称性和外加磁场打破反铁磁子晶格等效性，将动态不对称性转化为电荷电流的有限自旋极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁体因其无净磁化和超快自旋动力学而成为自旋电子学的有吸引力的平台，但其固有的自旋补偿电子结构传统上限制了它们在自旋输运中的主动作用。本文旨在探索在共线反铁磁体中实现自旋极化电荷输运的机制。

Method: 使用掺杂反铁磁Hubbard模型在动力学平均场理论框架下进行研究。分析电子关联如何在半满填充之外产生强自旋依赖散射，以及均匀磁场如何解除强制自旋简并输运的剩余对称性。

Result: 电子关联在掺杂时产生强自旋依赖散射，外加磁场解除反铁磁子晶格等效性。只有同时通过掺杂打破粒子-空穴对称性和通过外加磁场打破反铁磁子晶格等效性，才能将这些动态不对称性转化为电荷电流的有限自旋极化。

Conclusion: 电子关联是反铁磁自旋电子学的活性成分，揭示了在结构常规的共线反铁磁体中实现自旋极化输运的相关模拟对称性破缺机制，类似于交变磁体中的对称性破缺机制。

Abstract: Antiferromagnets offer an attractive platform for spintronics due to their absence of net magnetization and ultrafast spin dynamics, yet their intrinsically spin-compensated electronic structure has traditionally limited their active role in spin transport. Here we identify a minimal, correlation-driven route to spin-polarized charge transport in collinear antiferromagnets. Using the doped antiferromagnetic Hubbard model within dynamical mean-field theory, we show that electronic correlations generate strong spin-dependent scattering upon doping away from half filling, while a uniform magnetic field lifts the residual symmetries that enforce spin-degenerate transport. Only the combined breaking of particle--hole symmetry by doping and of the antiferromagnetic sublattice equivalence by the applied magnetic field converts these dynamical asymmetries into a finite spin polarization of the charge current. Our results establish electronic correlations as an active ingredient for antiferromagnetic spintronics and reveal a correlated analogue of the symmetry-breaking mechanism underlying altermagnetic spin-polarized transport in structurally conventional, collinear antiferromagnets.

</details>


### [127] [Quasiparticle properties below coherence onset in YbAl3 nanostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16563)
*Dale T. Lowder,Gage Eichman,Yuxin Wan,Karthik Rao,Ruiwen Xie,Hongbin Zhang,Debjoty Paul,Shouvik Chatterjee,Darrell G. Schlom,Kyle Shen,Emilia Morosan,Douglas Natelson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究利用介观输运测量技术探索强关联金属YbAl₃中的准粒子性质，通过纳米线器件观测到弱反局域化磁阻和普适电导涨落，并分析约翰逊-奈奎斯特噪声揭示显著的电子-声子能量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介观输运测量在探索关联金属中准粒子及其性质方面尚未得到充分研究。YbAl₃作为混合价化合物，其单离子近藤温度为670K，但热力学和输运性质表明重费米子准粒子在约37K开始相干。为了表征这些准粒子，需要采用适用于弱关联导体的介观技术。

Method: 使用外延薄膜制备的纳米线器件进行介观输运测量。具体包括：1) 观测弱反局域化磁阻和普适电导涨落以确定电子相干长度；2) 分析约翰逊-奈奎斯特噪声随偏置电流的变化，在多种公认模型框架下研究电子-声子能量损失。

Result: 实验发现：1) 纳米线器件中观测到弱反局域化磁阻和普适电导涨落，表明电子相干长度达数十纳米；2) 噪声测量分析揭示显著的电子-声子能量损失，且该损失随温度降低而增加；3) 这些结果在YbAl₃更广泛的性质背景下得到解释。

Conclusion: 介观输运技术成功应用于强关联金属YbAl₃的准粒子表征，揭示了其电子相干性和显著的电子-声子相互作用。该方法为探索关联金属中的准粒子性质提供了新途径，特别是在重费米子相干温度附近的行为。

Abstract: Mesoscopic transport measurements are underexplored as probes of quasiparticles and their properties in correlated metals. The mixed valence compound YbAl$_3$ exhibits a single-ion Kondo temperature of 670 K, while thermodynamic and transport properties (probed with specific heat, magnetic susceptibility, Hall effect, and resistivity) imply the onset of coherence of heavy fermion quasiparticles at T$* \approx$ 37 K. To characterize these quasiparticles, we utilize mesoscopic techniques familiar from weakly correlated conductors. In lithographically-defined nanowires etched from epitaxial films, we observe weak antilocalization magnetoresistance and universal conductance fluctuations, consistent with electronic coherence lengths of tens of nanometers. Additionally, analysis of Johnson-Nyquist noise measurements as a function of bias current reveal, within the context of a range of accepted models, a significant electron-phonon energy loss that increases with decreasing temperature, a finding that we contextualize within the broader properties of YbAl$_3$.

</details>


### [128] [Discerning ground state and photoemission-induced spin textures in altermagnetic $α$-MnTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16635)
*D. A. Usanov,S. W. D'Souza,A. Dal Din,J. Krempaský,F. Guo,O. J. Amin,C. Polley,M. Leandersson,G. Carbone,B. Thiagarajan,T. Jungwirth,L. Šmejkal,J. Minár,P. Wadley,J. H. Dil*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了反铁磁材料α-MnTe中自旋和角度分辨光电子能谱（SARPES）的挑战，重点关注光电子极化的起源和线性偏振光的选择规则异常行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁材料为实现非常规补偿磁性相提供了物理实现，在自旋电子学、拓扑和关联量子材料领域引起广泛研究兴趣。SARPES有望解析反铁磁能带结构中与实空间自旋序交织的动量依赖自旋纹理，但相关研究面临挑战。

Method: 以α-MnTe反铁磁能带结构中的相对论性d波状自旋极化为研究对象，结合实验测量和理论计算。实验采用自旋和角度分辨光电子能谱（SARPES），理论计算结合第一性原理能带结构和一步光电子发射计算。

Result: 研究发现使用线性偏振光时光电子发射选择规则表现出异常行为。成功区分了源自样品基态自旋纹理的光电子极化和源自光电子发射过程本身的极化。实验观测与理论计算结果一致。

Conclusion: 该研究为反铁磁材料的SARPES研究提供了重要见解，揭示了光电子极化起源的复杂性，并建立了区分基态自旋纹理极化和光电子过程极化的方法，对反铁磁材料的实验表征具有重要意义。

Abstract: Recently discovered altermagnets provide a physical realization of an unconventional compensated magnetic phases with a higher partial-wave type of ordering, reminiscent of unconventional superfluid phases. Their stability under normal conditions has sparked significant research interest, spanning fields from spintronics to topological and correlated quantum materials. Spin- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (SARPES) has great promise to resolve the momentum-dependent spin textures intricately interweaved with the altermagnetic real space spin order. Using the relativistic $d$-wave-like spin polarization on one of the nodal surfaces of the altermagnetic band structure of $α$-MnTe as an example, we here identify and resolve the challenges associated with (S)ARPES studies on altermagnets and offer insights into data interpretation. We focus particularly on the role of photoemission-induced electron polarization and the coupling between light and the Néel vector of a magnetic domain. Our findings reveal an extraordinary behaviour of photoemission selection rules while using linearly-polarized light. We observe, and distinguish, polarization of photoelectrons originating from the sample's ground state spin texture, on one hand, and from the photoemission process, on the other hand. Our experimental results are supported by a combination of ab initio band-structure and 1-step photoemission calculations.

</details>


### [129] [Machine Learning Reconstruction of High-Dimensional Electronic Structure from Angle-Resolved Photoemission Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16725)
*Yu Zhang,Yong Zhong,Nhat Huy Tran,Shuyi Li,Kyuho Lee,Yonghun Lee,Tiffany C. Wang,Harold Y. Hwang,Zhi-Xun Shen,Chunjing Jia*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 深度学习框架利用隐式神经表示从光电子能谱数据中加速提取哈密顿量参数，在过渡金属氧化物中表现优于传统拟合方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料的电子结构决定其涌现行为，可通过光电子能谱技术探测，但当前从高维光谱数据中定量提取哈密顿量参数仍依赖劳动密集、专家依赖的分析，缺乏标准化流程

Method: 引入基于隐式神经表示的深度学习框架，用于从光电子能谱数据中提取哈密顿量参数，应用于钙钛矿镍酸盐和锰酸盐两类过渡金属氧化物

Result: 该方法优于传统解析拟合程序，与实验费米面拓扑结构和能量-动量色散曲线的一致性更好

Conclusion: 深度学习工具能够弥合理论与实验之间的差距，为量子材料的高通量自主发现流程铺平道路

Abstract: The emergent behavior of quantum materials is governed by their electronic structure, which can be experimentally probed by photoemission spectroscopy techniques that generate a four-dimensional dataset of energy and momentum. However, the quantitative extraction of Hamiltonian parameters from these high-dimensional spectra remains a significant challenge, currently relying on labor-intensive, expert-dependent analysis rather than standardized workflows. Here, we introduce a deep learning framework based on implicit neural representations to accelerate the retrieval of Hamiltonian parameters in two types of transition-metal oxides: perovskite nickelates and manganites. Our approach outperforms traditional analytical fitting procedures, yielding superior agreement with experimental Fermi surface topologies and energy-momentum dispersions. This work highlights the potential of deep learning tools to bridge the gap between theory and experiment, paving the way for high-throughput, autonomous discovery pipelines in quantum materials.

</details>

### [130] [Extended Admissible Dissections of Marked Surfaces and Piano Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15966)
*Marina Godinho,Dave Murphy*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 论文引入了扩展可容许剖分的概念，并构造了相应的微分分次代数（钢琴代数），证明了其与A∞型离散丛范畴的完备化版本中经典生成元的微分分次自同态环拟同构，并建立了派生等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Amiot-Plamondon-Schroll的可容许剖分概念，构建微分分次代数作为温和代数的微分分次类似物，研究标记曲面上的代数结构与离散丛范畴之间的联系。

Method: 引入扩展可容许剖分概念，为每个这样的剖分构造微分分次代数（钢琴代数），利用作者先前结果建立加性等价关系，证明拟同构和派生等价性。

Result: 对于无穿孔的标记圆盘，钢琴代数与A∞型离散丛范畴完备化版本中经典生成元的微分分次自同态环拟同构；建立了加性等价关系；证明了同胚标记圆盘对应的钢琴代数派生等价。

Conclusion: 扩展可容许剖分及其对应的钢琴代数为研究标记曲面上的代数结构提供了新工具，建立了与离散丛范畴的深刻联系，为理解派生等价性提供了新视角。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of extended admissible dissections of a marked surface, building upon the notion of an admissible dissection of a marked surface by Amiot--Plamondon--Schroll. For each extended admissible dissection we construct a differential graded algebra, called a piano algebra, which may be viewed in some sense as a differential graded analogue of a gentle algebra. We show that for a marked disc without punctures, a piano algebra is quasi-isomorphic to the graded endomorphism ring of a classical generator of the Paquette--Yıldırım completion of the discrete cluster category of Dynkin type $A_{\infty}$, labelled $\overline{\mathcal{C}}_n$.
  We use previous results of the authors to show that there exists an additive equivalence between $\overline{\mathcal{C}}_n$ and the perfect derived category of a specific piano algebra, that sends triangles with two indecomposable terms to triangles with two indecomposable terms. We use this equivalence to prove that any two piano algebras coming from homeomorphic marked discs are derived equivalent.

</details>


### [131] [Relating Arthur packets of real unitary groups and $p$-adic symplectic and orthogonal groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16314)
*Taiwang Deng,Chang Huang,Bin Xu,Qixian Zhao*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文建立了实酉群与p进辛群或正交群之间某些Arthur包之间的显式对应关系，使得可以通过p进侧的结果计算实酉群的Arthur包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究实酉群与p进辛群或正交群之间Arthur包的对应关系，以便利用p进侧已知结果来计算实酉群的Arthur包，从而简化实酉群表示论中的计算问题。

Method: 主要方法包括：1) 建立Zuckerman平移函子（实侧）与Jacquet函子（p进侧）之间的显式关系；2) 构造具有固定无穷小特征的Langlands参数栈在相关实群与p进群之间的对应关系；3) 通过这种对应关系建立Arthur包的对应。

Result: 成功建立了实酉群与p进辛群或正交群之间某些Arthur包的显式对应关系，使得可以通过p进侧的结果计算实酉群的Arthur包。同时建立了Kazhdan-Lusztig多项式和微局部分析在实侧与p进侧之间的关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为实酉群表示论提供了新的计算方法，通过建立与p进群的对应关系，简化了实酉群Arthur包的计算，并揭示了实侧与p进侧表示论之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We establish an explicit correspondence of certain Arthur packets between real unitary groups and $p$-adic symplectic or orthogonal groups. This allows one to compute Arthur packets of real unitary groups by translating results from the $p$-adic side. A main ingredient in our proof is an explicit relation between Zuckerman's translation functor on the real side and the Jacquet functor on the $p$-adic side. To achieve this, we construct a correspondence of stacks of Langlands parameters with fixed infinitesimal characters between the relevant real and $p$-adic groups. Our approach also allows one to relate the Kazhdan-Lusztig polynomials and the microlocal geometry between real and $p$-adic sides.

</details>


### [132] [On the depth of the adjoint representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16355)
*Arindam Jana,Amiya Mondal*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了非阿基米德局部域上Carayol表示的伴随表示的斜率计算问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在局部朗兰兹对应中，GL_n(F)光滑表示的深度与局部伽罗瓦表示的斜率是对应的不变量。对于不可约伽罗瓦表示，斜率与Swan导子有良好关系，但对于可约表示（如Carayol表示的伴随表示），其行为较为微妙，需要明确的计算公式。

Method: 针对奇剩余特征p的非阿基米德局部域F，研究Carayol表示的伴随表示的斜率。通过分析可约伽罗瓦表示的结构特性，推导出斜率的显式计算公式。

Result: 给出了Carayol表示的伴随表示的斜率的显式计算公式，为理解可约伽罗瓦表示在局部朗兰兹对应中的行为提供了具体工具。

Conclusion: 本文成功计算了Carayol表示伴随表示的斜率，填补了可约伽罗瓦表示斜率计算的理论空白，深化了对局部朗兰兹对应中不变量关系的理解。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a non-archimedean local field of odd residual characteristic $p$. The depth of a smooth representation of ${\rm GL}_n(F)$ is an invariant of Local Langlands Correspondence (LLC). The analogous notion on the Galois side of LLC is known as the slope of a local Galois representation. The slope is well related to the Swan conductor for irreducible Galois representations, whereas its behavior is subtle for reducible Galois representations. In this article, we provide an explicit formula for the slope of the adjoint of a Carayol representation of the local Galois group.

</details>


### [133] [Classification of degenerate Verma modules over $E(4,4)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16507)
*Nicoletta Cantarini,Fabrizio Caselli,Victor Kac*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文完成了对线性紧致李超代数E(4,4)上退化Verma模的分类，从而完成了所有例外线性紧致李超代数上Verma模的完整描述


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完成对例外线性紧致李超代数上Verma模的完整分类，特别是针对E(4,4)代数，这是该系列研究的最后一步

Method: 对线性紧致李超代数E(4,4)上的退化Verma模进行分类研究，延续了先前对其他例外代数类似研究的方法

Result: 成功分类了E(4,4)上的退化Verma模，完成了所有例外线性紧致李超代数上Verma模的完整描述，发现退化模及其同态构成无限双边复形，可视为de Rham复形的推广

Conclusion: 该研究完成了例外线性紧致李超代数上Verma模的完整分类，揭示了退化模构成的无限双边复形结构，为de Rham复形提供了新的推广形式

Abstract: In this paper we classify degenerate Verma modules over the linearly compact Lie superalgebra $E(4,4)$. This completes the description of Verma modules over the exceptional linearly compact Lie superalgebras. As in the other cases all degenerate modules and morphisms between them give rise to infinite bilateral complexes which may be viewed as a generalization of de Rham complexes.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Fourier transform of irregular connections on $\mathbb P^1$ and classification of Argyres-Douglas theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15942)
*Jean Douçot*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文从数学角度解释了A型Argyres-Douglas理论的对偶性，通过野非阿贝尔霍奇对应，将这些理论的对偶实现为傅里叶变换和莫比乌斯变换的组合操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Beem等人最近发现的A型Argyres-Douglas理论对偶性提供数学解释，这些对偶性基于Xie的工作。利用野非阿贝尔霍奇对应，将理论定义数据转化为P^1上特定形式的不规则联络的奇点数据。

Method: 使用野非阿贝尔霍奇对应，将A型Argyres-Douglas理论映射到P^1上的不规则联络奇点数据。证明所有对偶性都可以通过两种基本操作的组合实现：傅里叶变换和交换零与无穷远的莫比乌斯变换。证明依赖于给出傅里叶变换奇点数据显式表达的稳相公式。

Result: 成功将A型Argyres-Douglas理论的对偶性解释为傅里叶变换和莫比乌斯变换的组合操作。阐明了描述A型Argyres-Douglas理论3d镜像的箭图与Boalch-Yamakawa及作者工作中定义的非阿贝尔霍奇图之间的关系：3d镜像对应于在基本操作轨道中唯一没有负边/负环的非阿贝尔霍奇图。

Conclusion: A型Argyres-Douglas理论的对偶性可以通过傅里叶变换和莫比乌斯变换这两种基本操作的组合来统一理解。这一数学框架不仅解释了已知对偶性，还揭示了3d镜像与奇点数据轨道中特定非阿贝尔霍奇图之间的对应关系。

Abstract: We give a mathematical interpretation of the dualities between type $A$ Argyres-Douglas theories recently obtained by Beem, Martone, Sacchi, Singh and Stedman, building on work of Xie. Using the fact that, via the wild nonabelian Hodge correspondence, the data defining such a theory amount to singularity data for irregular connections on $\mathbb P^1$ of a specific form, we show that these dualities can all be realized as compositions of two types of more basic operations acting on such irregular connections: the Fourier transform and a Möbius transformation exchanging zero and infinity. The proof relies on the stationary phase formula giving explicit expressions for the singularity data of the Fourier transform. We also clarify the relation between the quivers describing the 3d mirrors of type $A$ Argyres-Douglas theories and the nonabelian Hodge diagrams defined in work of Boalch-Yamakawa and of the author: the 3d mirror corresponds to the unique nonabelian Hodge diagram with no negative edges/loops among those of singularity data in the corresponding orbit under basic operations.

</details>


### [135] [BC Toda chain I: reflection operator and eigenfunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16380)
*N. Belousov,S. Derkachov,S. Khoroshkin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文获得了BC型边界相互作用量子Toda链本征函数的高斯-吉文塔尔积分表示，引入了满足反射方程的反射算子，并定义了BC Toda链的巴克斯特算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有BC型边界相互作用的量子Toda链的本征函数表示问题，扩展经典Toda链理论到边界相互作用情形，为这类可积系统提供新的数学工具和表示方法。

Method: 引入满足反射方程的反射算子（与DST链Lax矩阵相关），定义BC Toda链的巴克斯特算子，证明其与哈密顿量的对易性，并推导相应的巴克斯特方程。

Result: 获得了BC型边界相互作用量子Toda链本征函数的高斯-吉文塔尔积分表示，建立了反射算子与巴克斯特算子的理论框架，证明了相关算子的对易关系。

Conclusion: 成功构建了BC型边界相互作用量子Toda链的完整数学框架，包括本征函数表示、反射算子和巴克斯特算子理论，为这类可积系统的进一步研究提供了基础工具。

Abstract: We obtain Gauss-Givental integral representation for the eigenfunctions of quantum Toda chain with boundary interaction of BC type. For this we introduce reflection operator satisfying reflection equation with DST chain Lax matrices. Besides, we define Baxter operators for BC Toda chain, prove their commutativity with Hamiltonians and derive the corresponding Baxter equation.

</details>


### [136] [BC Toda chain II: symmetries. Dual picture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16387)
*N. Belousov,S. Derkachov,S. Khoroshkin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了BC Toda链量子模型中Baxter算符的可交换性，构建了具有谱参数对称性的波函数，并建立了与超八面体Whittaker函数的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前一篇论文中，作者推导了量子BC Toda链波函数的高斯-吉文塔尔积分表示，并引入了该模型的Baxter算符。本文旨在进一步研究这些算符的性质和波函数的数学结构。

Method: 1. 证明Baxter算符的可交换性；2. 证明构造的波函数在谱参数的带符号置换下具有对称性；3. 推导波函数的梅林-巴恩斯积分表示；4. 利用该表示证明波函数满足关于谱参数的对偶差分方程组；5. 建立波函数与超八面体Whittaker函数的等价关系；6. 给出波函数正交性和完备性的启发式证明。

Result: 1. Baxter算符被证明是可交换的；2. 波函数对角化Baxter算符；3. 获得了波函数的梅林-巴恩斯积分表示；4. 证明了波函数满足对偶差分方程组；5. 建立了波函数与超八面体Whittaker函数的对应关系；6. 提供了波函数正交性和完备性的启发式证明。

Conclusion: 本文系统研究了量子BC Toda链的数学结构，建立了Baxter算符的可交换性，证明了波函数的对称性质，并通过梅林-巴恩斯积分表示将其与超八面体Whittaker函数联系起来，为模型的完整数学描述奠定了基础。

Abstract: In the previous paper we derived Gauss-Givental integral representation for the wave functions of quantum BC Toda chain and also introduced Baxter operators for this model. In the present paper we prove commutativity of Baxter operators, as well as show that the constructed wave functions are symmetric with respect to signed permutations of spectral parameters and diagonalize Baxter operators. Furthermore, we derive Mellin-Barnes integral representation for the wave functions. With its help we show that wave functions satisfy dual system of difference equations with respect to spectral parameters and coincide with hyperoctahedral Whittaker functions. Finally, we give heuristic proofs of orthogonality and completeness of the wave functions.

</details>


### [137] [A symplectic geometric origin of universal quartic modified dispersion relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16532)
*Sanjib Dey,Mir Faizal*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文证明在量子引力相关的极小运动学假设下，四次修正的相对论色散关系普遍从形变量子化相空间中产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子引力背景下相对论色散关系的修正起源，探索形变量子化相空间如何自然产生普朗克尺度修正。

Method: 采用三种独立方法：Fedosov-Berezin量子化、谱几何和拓扑斯理论表述，在满足积分辛结构、相容近复结构和规范不变二形式扇区的运动学假设下进行分析。

Result: 所有三种方法都得到相同的四次修正，该修正由单个几何长度尺度控制，揭示了这种修正的普遍性起源。

Conclusion: 四次相对论色散关系修正在形变量子化相空间中具有普遍性，其普朗克尺度修正由基本几何结构决定，三种不同数学框架的一致性强化了这一结论。

Abstract: We show that quartic modifications of relativistic dispersion relations arise generically from deformation-quantized phase spaces under minimal kinematical assumptions relevant to quantum gravity. When the kinematics admits an integral symplectic structure, a compatible almost-complex structure, and a gauge-invariant two-form sector, the leading Planck-scale correction is controlled by a single geometric length scale. We establish this result through three independent approaches: Fedosov-Berezin quantization, spectral geometry, and a topos-theoretic formulation, all of which yield the same quartic correction and clarify the origin of its apparent universality.

</details>


### [138] [The weakly interacting tenfold way](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16799)
*Lucas C. P. A. M. Müssnich,Renato Vasconcellos Vieira*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 通过不可约自由费米子系统的时间演化算子实现拓扑K理论谱KU和KO，并证明弱相互作用下的十重分类稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立自由费米子系统与拓扑K理论之间的具体联系，为凝聚态物理中的拓扑相分类提供严格的数学框架，并研究弱相互作用对拓扑分类稳定性的影响

Method: 使用具有对称性的不可约自由费米子系统的时间演化算子实现KU和KO谱，明确构造结构悬垂映射；引入弱相互作用时间演化算子的几何定义，证明相关谱KU^{wi}和KO^{wi}可形变收缩到标准谱

Result: 成功实现了KU和KO谱的具体构造，证明了弱相互作用下十重分类的稳定性，为拓扑相在相互作用存在时的鲁棒性提供了严格的同伦论证明

Conclusion: 自由费米子系统的时间演化算子为拓扑K理论提供了物理实现，弱相互作用不会破坏十重分类的拓扑结构，这为拓扑物态在现实材料中的稳定性提供了理论基础

Abstract: We present implementations of the topological K-theory spectra $KU$ and $KO$ in terms of time evolution operators of irreducible free fermion systems with symmetries, with explicit formulas for the structural suspension maps. We also introduce a geometric definition of weakly interacting time evolution operators, and show how associated spectra $KU^{wi}$ and $KO^{wi}$ deformation retract to $KU$ and $KO$. We thus have a stable homotopy theoretical proof that the tenfold way is stable to weak interactions.

</details>


### [139] [A dense focusing Ablowitz-Ladik soliton gas and its asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16696)
*Meisen Chen,Engui Fan,Zhaoyu Wang,Yiling Yang,Lun Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了聚焦Ablowitz-Ladik系统的孤子气体解，该解定义为N-孤子解的大N极限，源于虚轴上两个不相交区间内极点连续谱的累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究聚焦Ablowitz-Ladik系统的孤子气体解，探索从离散孤子解到连续谱极限的数学描述，建立此类非线性系统的新类型解。

Method: 将孤子气体解定义为N-孤子解的大N极限，该解源于虚轴上两个不相交区间内极点连续谱的累积。通过Fredholm行列式表示该解，并利用Riemann-Hilbert特征化方法进行分析。

Result: 建立了孤子气体解的Fredholm行列式表示，通过Riemann-Hilbert特征化推导了t=0时的大空间渐近行为以及解的大时间渐近行为。

Conclusion: 成功构造了聚焦Ablowitz-Ladik系统的孤子气体解，提供了其数学表示并分析了渐近行为，为研究此类非线性系统的连续谱极限解奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a soliton gas solution for the focusing Ablowitz-Ladik system. This solution is defined as the large N limit of the N-soliton solution, and arises from a continuous spectrum of poles that accumulate within two disjoint intervals on the imaginary axis. We show that this gas solution admits a Fredholm determinant representation. By further exploring its Riemann-Hilbert characterization, we are able to establish the large-space asymptotics at t = 0 and large-time asymptotics of the gas solution.

</details>


### [140] [Ergodicity in discrete-time quantum walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16837)
*Kiran Kumar,Mostafa Sabri*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文分析了整数格上齐次离散时间量子行走的遍历性，在一维情况下建立了酉算子绝对连续谱与位置空间动力学均匀分布之间的完全等价关系，在高维情况下提出了"无重复图"准则来区分完全和部分遍历性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子行走的遍历性，特别是建立量子系统动力学与谱特性之间的精确关系，这在量子遍历性理论中具有重要意义。目前缺乏对量子行走均匀分布与谱特性之间系统联系的研究。

Method: 采用谱分析方法，研究齐次离散时间量子行走在整数格上的动力学。在一维情况下建立绝对连续谱与均匀分布的等价关系，在高维情况下引入"无重复图"准则来区分遍历性类型，并通过大量示例验证理论框架。

Result: 在一维情况下首次证明了量子行走酉算子的绝对连续谱与位置空间动力学均匀分布之间的完全等价关系。在高维情况下，提出了区分完全遍历性和部分遍历性的"无重复图"准则，并阐明了弱收敛与全变差收敛的区别。

Conclusion: 该研究为量子行走的遍历性提供了系统的理论框架，建立了谱特性与动力学行为之间的精确联系，特别是在一维情况下获得了完全等价关系，为量子遍历性理论的发展做出了重要贡献。

Abstract: We undertake a detailed analysis of ergodicity for homogeneous discrete-time quantum walks on the integer lattice. The most significant result of our paper holds in dimension one, and gives a complete equivalence between the absolutely continuous spectrum of the unitary operator encoding the walk, and the equidistribution of its dynamics in position space, which appears for the first time in the context of large-volume quantum ergodicity. In higher dimensions, we give a criterion for full and partial ergodicity in terms of a finer property of the spectrum which we dub ``No Repeating Graphs'', and distinguish how strongly the equidistribution is taking place (weak convergence vs total variation). The paper includes a wealth of examples where we apply our criteria, with certain families of walks fully characterized.

</details>


### [141] [Solving gravitational field equations by Wiener-Hopf matrix factorisation, and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16847)
*M. Cristina Câmara,Gabriel Lopes Cardoso*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文综述了将二维爱因斯坦场方程视为可积系统，通过单值矩阵的Wiener-Hopf分解同时获得场方程及其Lax对的精确解，展示了引力场方程、可积系统、Riemann-Hilbert问题与Wiener-Hopf分解理论之间的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在回顾和展示引力场方程与可积系统理论之间的深刻联系，特别是通过Wiener-Hopf分解技术来获得精确解。作者强调了过去十年中由于奇异积分方程和Toeplitz算子研究带来的Wiener-Hopf分解技术进步，使得这一领域取得了重要发展。

Method: 采用跨学科方法，结合广义相对论、复分析和算子理论。核心方法是将二维爱因斯坦场方程视为可积系统，通过单值矩阵的规范Wiener-Hopf分解来同时获得场方程及其Lax对的精确解。特别强调了τ-不变性性质推广的Wiener-Hopf分解作为一种新的解生成方法。

Result: 通过具体实例展示了Wiener-Hopf分解如何产生引力理论场方程的显式精确解。证明了τ-不变性性质推广的Wiener-Hopf分解提供了一种新的解生成方法。建立了引力场方程、可积系统、Riemann-Hilbert问题和Wiener-Hopf分解理论之间的系统联系。

Conclusion: 本文论证了基于广义相对论、复分析和算子理论的跨学科方法对于研究引力场方程的重要性。Wiener-Hopf分解技术不仅提供了获得精确解的有效途径，其通过τ-不变性性质的推广还开辟了新的解生成方法，展示了数学物理不同领域之间深刻的相互联系。

Abstract: By viewing Einstein's field equations -- reduced to two dimensions -- as an integrable system, one can simultaneously obtain exact solutions to both the equations themselves and their associated Lax pair via a canonical Wiener-Hopf factorisation of a so-called monodromy matrix. In this article, we review this remarkable interplay between gravitational field equations, integrable systems, Riemann-Hilbert problems, and Wiener-Hopf factorisation theory, with particular emphasis on developments from the past decade enabled by advances in Wiener-Hopf factorisation techniques arising from the study of singular integral equations and Toeplitz operators. Through a variety of concrete examples, we illustrate how Wiener-Hopf factorisation yields explicit, exact solutions to the field equations of gravitational theories, and how its generalisation through a so-called $τ$-invariance property provides a new solution-generating method. Along the way, we aim to demonstrate the importance of an interdisciplinary approach -- grounded in General Relativity, Complex Analysis, and Operator Theory -- for the study of gravitational field equations.

</details>
